## Document 024: 2026 has exposed new risks for the European gas market (2026-11-09)

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026 has exposed new risks for the European gas market (2026-11-09).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank

economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026EDITORIAL
2026 HAS EXPOSED NEW RISKS FOR THE EUROPEAN GAS MARKET
1 C. Grekou, V. Mignon, L. Ragot, 2026, ‘From Russian gas to the global LNG market: The European Union’s new vulnerability’, CEPII, Policy Brief.
2 ACER, 2026, ‘Analysis of the European LNG market development. Monitoring report’.
3 These contracts allow the buyer to choose, or change, the delivery point for the shipment. Recent US contracts (Free On Board and flexible destination) generally

feature these characteristics.
4 IEA, 2026, ‘Global Gas Security Review & Gas Market Report Q4-2026’.
Europe’s vulnerability to the gas market has changed in recent years. Whilst its reliance on imports has remained stable,
its gas supply has been more vulnerable to global market conditions since 2022, due to a shift in the composition of its
imports. Furthermore, in 2026, the combination of high geopolitical risk in one of the main production regions and the

pressure of climate change on European consumers has created an unprecedented situation, leading to price pressures
and significant uncertainty regarding the short-term outlook. Even if they ease, tensions in the European gas market could
persist beyond the winter, largely due to the need to refill inventories.
Whilst Europe is not heavily reliant on gas imports from the Middle
East, it has had to contend with a fresh surge in prices since the out -

break of war in Iran last February, a rise that has, in part, been passed
on to electricity prices this summer. Furthermore, new sources of
vulnerability, particularly those linked to climate change, are at play.
These call for caution regarding the short-term outlook for the Euro -
pean gas market.
EUROPE’S GROWING EXPOSURE TO MARKET VOLATILITY
Unlike the 2022 energy crisis, which hit Europe directly, the war in
Iran is indirectly affecting the European gas market. 80% of gas exports

from the Middle East are destined for Asia. However, the growing inter-
connection between the two main gas-importing regions — Asia and
Europe — has resulted in the crisis spreading to Europe. Since 2022,
the transmission of shocks to liquefied natural gas (LNG) benchmark
prices on the European and Asian markets (TTF and JKM) has increased
significantly, in both directions1.
Despite a fall of around 20% in European gas consumption since 2022,
the replacement of around 60% of Russian pipeline imports with pur -

chases of liquefied natural gas (see Chart 1) has kept Europe’s vulne -
rability at a high level. Given developments in the LNG market —
growing interconnection between European and Asian markets and
the importance of spot contracts (around one-third of European supply
contracts2) — Europe finds itself, through no fault of its own, increa -
singly exposed to gas price volatility.
Furthermore, the increasing autonomy and flexibility of the global
LNG market may exacerbate this volatility. The higher proportion of

LNG sales contracts with flexible destinations3 (rising from 29% to 51%
of the total between 2016 and 2025), linked to the massive influx of
US LNG onto the market, together with the reduction in the share of
contracts indexed to oil prices (from 70% to 55% over the same period),
have made price formation on the LNG market more autonomous, the-
reby increasing the opportunities for arbitrage and the flexibility of this
market.
MAJOR DISRUPTIONS IN THE LNG MARKET HAVE CONSTRAINED THE REFIL-

LING OF EUROPEAN INVENTORIES
The 85% reduction in LNG exports from Qatar and the UAE in Q2 and Q3
2026 was offset by two-thirds through, on the one hand, an increase
in exports from other producing countries (primarily the United States)
and, on the other hand, a fall in demand in Asia and Europe (which
is forecast to reach, respectively, 1.5% and 3% year-on-year in 2026,
according to IEA forecasts4). Owing to the damage caused by the war to
infrastructure (17% of Qatar’s production capacity is out of service) and

logistical constraints specific to the LNG market (unavailability of LNG
carriers), the LNG market has benefited only marginally from the re -
sumption of flows through the Strait of Hormuz since July. Against this
backdrop of tensions in the gas market, high prices in Europe (up by
around 174% since the outbreak of the conflict) have significantly
slowed the refilling of European stocks. They stood at 75% of full capa-
city at the end of October, whilst the official target is 80% (see Chart 2).

OVER THE WINTER, DIVERGENT FACTORS COULD STABILISE PRICES AT A
HIGH LEVEL
Gas stock levels that are below the target set by the European Commis-
sion do not increase the risk of a shortage on the European continent.
Nevertheless, Europe’s vulnerability towards the end of winter, in the
event of a cold snap, would force it to buy on the market at high prices.

Furthermore, from a technical perspective, exposure to the conse -
quences of a cold snap at the end of winter is all the more pronounced
as the rate at which gas can be withdrawn from storage (the ease with
which a country can access its supplies) decreases (due to lower stock
levels in storage facilities).
Apart from stock levels at the start of winter, three factors will deter -

mine the situation at the end of the season, a key period of vulnera -
bility:
1/ the resumption of exports from the Gulf, a region that is essential for
restoring market balance in the short term;
2/ weather conditions during the winter;
3/ renewable energy production.
According to the IEA, in the worst-case scenario (with exports from the
Middle East only resuming from the end of the first quarter of 2027, a
cold winter and low renewable energy production), stocks would stand

at 7% of total capacity by the end of winter, compared with 33% in
the best-case scenario. Such levels remain significantly below the ave-
rage for the last five years at this time of year: 80% in the worst-case
scenario and 18% in the best-case scenario. This would underpin res -
tocking demand over the next two quarters, and therefore keep prices
at a fairly high level. For the time being, a significant recovery in LNG
exports from the Gulf remains highly uncertain, even though indirect

talks between the warring parties have resumed. On a more positive
note, the current development of a very powerful El Niño event should,
in principle, be associated with relatively mild temperatures during the
winter in western Europe. According to the futures markets, European
gas prices are not expected to return to their pre-crisis levels until
2028 (see Chart 3).
ARE WE HEADING FOR A SUMMER PEAK IN GAS DEMAND?
In addition to these factors, which are likely to prolong market tensions

during the first half of 2027, the summer period could also be cha -
racterised by increased demand for gas. During the summer of 2026,
5 In early October 2026, the water levels in French reservoirs reached a ten-year low, standing at 23% below seasonal averages.
6 RTE data.
7 M. Mueller, P. Devaux, October 2026, ‘What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity
continues to turn the screw?’, EcoInsight.

8 Oxford Institute for Energy Studies, October 2026, Quarterly Gas Market Review.
weather conditions hampered the generation of certain forms of re -
newable energy (heatwaves and droughts restricting electricity gene -
ration from hydroelectric5 and wind power sources). They also disrup-
ted nuclear power generation (in France, nuclear electricity generation
fell by around 10% year-on-year between August and October6). Along-
side these constraints on carbon-free electricity generation, heatwaves

are driving up demand for electricity, particularly for air conditioning.
Against this backdrop, as a source of flexibility within the electricity
mix, gas remains a key determinant of electricity prices in Europe 7.
In Q3 2026, whilst the decline in European gas consumption mainly
affected industry, the residential sector and the commercial sector 8,
consumption by gas-fired power stations was 9% higher than in 2025.
STRENGTHENING EUROPEAN PROGRAMMES

In this context, and despite the progress made in decarbonising the
European electricity mix, high gas prices have led to a rise in whole -
sale electricity prices in most European countries. Between July and
September 2026, these prices were on average 54% higher than in the
same period a year earlier.
Against this backdrop of widespread rises in energy prices, European go-
vernments have primarily introduced measures to support households’
purchasing power when buying fuel. Measures relating to gas supply

remain fairly limited. The European Commission has extended the tar-
get for refilling European stocks to 80% (instead of 90%) for 2026. It has
also urged Member States to extend the stock-refill season in order
to smooth out purchase prices. Furthermore, the measures taken in
2022 under the RePower EU programme have been maintained: redu -
cing dependence on Russian gas with the aim of a complete phase-out
(pipeline and LNG) by the end of 2027; and pooling gas purchases at

European level. The ‘decarbonisation and electrification’ component of
this programme was strengthened in April 2026 by the Accelerate EU
programme, notably through measures to accelerate the electrification
of demand and the development of electricity grid infrastructure at
European level.

At national level, it is worth highlighting the price cap mechanism for
gas purchased by power stations, which the Italian government is set
to introduce. In 2025, gas set the price of electricity in Italy in 73% of
cases (compared with an average of 40% across Europe). This price cap
mechanism, similar to that introduced in Spain in 2022, is, in principle,

only sustainable in the short term. Indeed, its cost is likely to be signi-
ficant given the Italian electricity mix’s high dependence on gas.
*****
Increased market dependence and the consequences of rising climate
risk for European electricity generation are altering the vulnerabilities
associated with the continent’s gas market. The sources of risk are
increasing in number and becoming harder to manage. This calls for
the continuation and strengthening of decarbonisation efforts in the

European policies launched in 2022.
Pascal Devaux
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

Inventec (2356.TW)
10 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Inventec
Company description
Established in 1975 and listed in 1996, Taiwan-based Inventec is a key ODM
of NBs, servers, smartphones, and wearable/IoT devices to global customers.
Major shareholders include the founder Mr. Yeh Kuo-I, whose family has (both
directly and indirectly) a c35% stake.
Investment strategy
We rate Inventec shares as Buy. We expect stable demand in NB and server to
help support near-term earnings, while product diversification within smart

devices to areas such as IoT and medical space should help improve L-T
margins. We expect the company's solid cash yield to serve as a downside
cushion.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$65 for Inventec shares is based on 17x 2027 EPS, which
is slightly above the mid-point of its historical trading range, reflecting the
company's product diversification and stable demand outlook led by servers
and commercial NBs, as well as increasing AI server exposure that could

potentially drive margin expansion and better earnings momentum.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) Slower-than-expected shipment growth/weaker-than-expected
sell-through in HomePods and AirPods could lead to downside in our sales
forecasts; 2) If competitors adopt aggressive pricing strategies to gain market
shares, our ASP and margin assumptions could face downside risk; 3) Lower-
than-expected server margins driven by rising exposure to Chinese customers

could impact our earnings forecasts negatively; 4) Deterioration in NB
demand and investment in solar would hurt our forecasts; and 5) Stronger-
than-expected Taiwan dollar could lead to FX loss and downside to our
current forecasts.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

has a significantly shorter product lead time (see here), while Hiwin has implemented 
price hikes across its core products (including both BS and LG), potentially resulting in

### Page 3

Investment Thesis and Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment thesis - Hiwin Corp 
Hiwin is the second-largest global linear motion supplier with 18% market share in 2024. 
It continues to witness decelerating market share growth from 2020 when it started to 
pursue a deleveraging strategy.   
Although we expect Hiwin to continue to benefit from rising demand from the 
semiconductor/EV industry given the upgrading trend, we are Sell rated, given: (1) we 
expect a weak end-market demand recovery and high inventory levels for the near term, 
leading to high pricing pressure and a stagnant utilization rate; (2) continued 
overcapacity issues in mainland China, where the company has higher exposure (45% in 
2024) vs. peers such as THK (24%), and we expect this could lead to further 
deterioration in Hiwin’s revenue/profitability outlook in the near term; and (3) the 
negative impact from its long-term deleveraging strategy. Hiwin continues to pay back 
long-term debt and expand capacity to better compete with peers, which we believe will 
further impact EPS growth and valuation. The stock is currently trading above its 
historical average valuation (33.0x). We expect its P/E multiple to derate once the 
market begins to adjust for a prolonged downcycle and expect downgrades to 
consensus EPS growth/ROE estimates, suggesting high downside risk to Hiwin’s 
valuation.  
Price target risks and methodology - Hiwin Corp 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$180 is based on a target P/E of 20x 2027E 
EPS (in-line with Hiwin’s average downcycle valuation, or 1std lower than the historical 
average P/E for the past 10 years).  
Key upside risks: (1) Stronger-than-expected EV & semi CAPEX growth; (2) a favorable

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Auras Technology (3324.TWO)
11 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Auras Technology
Valuation
We value Auras using a PE-based approach, reflecting its accelerating earnings trajectory and rising share in liquid cooling
market of AI servers. Our TP of NT$2,000 is based on 20x 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified given the company’s
expanding server revenue base, deeper engagement with CSP customers, and broader product portfolio across cold plates,
CDMs, and CDUs.

Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model, but assigning a High Risk rating is not supported in our view by
other qualitative factors such as rapid RVL progress and Thailand expansion, and so a High Risk rating has not been applied.
Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price include: 1) slower adoption of liquid cooling; 2) heightened price
competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

M
Update
Quanta Computer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 OpM miss; stay OW
Management said AI demand has remained unchanged in recent
weeks. It reiterates confidence in strong double-digit y/y growth
into 2027. General server demand continues to exceed
expectations YTD, driven by agentic AI.
Platform transition weighs on 4Q shipments; VR200 to support CY27 growth: AI
servers contributed 75–80% of 3Q26 server revenue, with CY26 AI revenue on track
for triple-digit y/y growth and ~65% of Quanta’s total revenue. The platform

transition is expected to drive a q/q decline in 4Q26 NVL72 rack shipments, but
management’s tone suggests a milder decline than our prior forecast of 4K racks (vs.
~6.2K in 3Q26). We therefore raise our 4Q26 NVL72 production forecast 1K, to 5K,
implying 21.9K builds in CY26. Management expects shipments to accelerate in 1Q27
as VR200 ramps, supporting high double-digit AI server revenue growth in CY27.
ASIC servers’ revenue contribution is projected to double, from high single digits in

CY26 to double digits in CY27.
Agentic AI supports general server growth: Management noted that YTD general
server shipments have exceeded expectations, with both shipments and revenue
increasing q/q in 3Q26 and further growth expected in 4Q26, primarily driven by
agentic AI. Management expects steady double-digit y/y growth in both shipments
and revenue in CY27, with deployments broadly balanced between x86 and Arm-
based platforms and comparable margin profiles. While industry-wide component

shortages persist, particularly in memory, CPUs and PCBs, management indicated
that tier-1 CSPs continue to secure sufficient supply.
Lower NB volumes and rising costs pressure margins: Quanta’s NB shipments fell
17% q/q, to 9.6M units in 3Q26, weaker than normal seasonality but above guidance
of a 20% decline. Rising memory costs and lower scale weighed on profitability, and
we expect margin pressure to persist in 4Q26. Management guides for another

double-digit q/q shipment decline in 4Q26, albeit narrower than in 3Q26, implying a
15–20% y/y decline for CY26. We project 4Q26 shipments of 8.3M units (-14% q/q)
and CY26 shipments of 39.4M units (-15% y/y). For CY27, management expects NB
shipments to decline in high single digits to 10% y/y and PC/NB revenue to fall to a
single-digit share of the mix, reflecting AI server racks’ substantially higher ASPs.
Keep price target at NT$385 and stay OW: Our target implies 11.8x CY27e P/E or

11.0x CY28e P/E, vs. its three-year range of 6-18x. Risk-reward looks attractive at  9.1x
CY27e P/E, below the three-year average of 11.7x, with ~5.1% dividend yield (DPS of
NT$15.6).

### Page 4

M
Update
4
3Q26 Results Summary
Component cost pressure drove 3Q GM miss:
Revenue was NT$1,115.5bn (+8% q/q, +125% y/y), but GM deteriorated to 4.8% (-30bps q/
q, -210bps y/y), which is 40bps below our estimate and 20bps below the Street.
Management cited the  following reasons for the GM miss:
• PC/NB: cost pressures from inflationary component pricing; diminishing economies
of scale.
• Servers: inflationary component pricing pressured server gross margins, despite a

slight decline in AI server shipments.
However, opex was also higher than expected, mainly owing to higher R&D expenses.
Thus, OP came in at NT$32.2bn (-6% q/q, +75% y/y), 7%  below our estimate and 112%
below the Street.
OpM was 2.9% (-40bps q/q, -80bps y/y). Management guided for 2H OpM to be flat h/h,
so we think 3Q OpM was a miss.
Non-op income was higher than expected because of ~NT$3.9bn in FX gains, so net profit
came in at ~NT$28.4bn (-1% q/q, +73% y/y) for EPS of NT$7.36, 6% above our estimate

and 2% above the Street.
Consignment? Under the buy-and-sell model, high-ASP server rack procurement inflated
revenue but diluted gross margins and to a lesser extent operating margins (owing to
operating leverage) via pass-through component accounting. Management continues to
stress absolute profit expansion and sustained >20% ROIC over margin ratios. To mitigate
balance sheet inventory carrying risk and working capital burden, Quanta will continue to

negotiate for a consignment business model with its CSP customers into CY27.

Estimate Changes
We update our model to factor in the 3Q26 margin miss and updated guidance from
management, which leads to increases of 7%, 13%, and 13% changes to our EPS estimates
for 2026, 2027, and 2028, respectively. We primarily raise our revenue assumptions while
lowering our margin assumptions for 2026-2028.

### Page 6

M
Update
6
Price Target and Valuation Methodology
We keep our price target at NT$385, reflecting the offset between raised 2026-28
earnings estimates and a lower medium-term growth rate: This implies 11.8x CY27e and
11.0x CY28e EPS, in line with the average of Quanta's three-year historical range of 11.7x.
We view this as reasonable because we expect the company to benefit from rising AI
server and EV demand over the long term, which will offset the weakness in the PC

segment and drive mix improvements.
We continue to apply a residual income model to derive our price target. Our key
assumptions include:
• A cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of 1.2, equity premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of
1.5%)
• A 7.0% medium-term growth rate – lowered from 8.5% previously, as we bake in
some conservatism on concerns over a potential slowdown in data center build-
out following the US midterm elections (see our Global Team's view), which could

affect Quanta's server business directly.
• A 3% terminal growth rate
Our bull and bear case scenario values also stay unchanged at $545 and NT$230,
respectively.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

1 群益投顧 
精材(3374 TT)與 台 積 電 (2330 TT)簽 訂 先 進 封 裝 oS 產 能 外 包 協 議 ， 深 化 策 略 合 作 。 精
材 此 次 取 得 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 產 能 外 包 合 作 ， 對 長 期 業 務 結 構 升 級 具 正 面 意 義 ，
惟 股 價 於 公 告 前 已 連 續 6 個 交 易 日 上 漲 ， 累 計 漲 幅 約 40%，11/13/2026 收盤達 519 元
漲 停 ， 本 益 比 已 達 43 倍 ， 加 上 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年才開始第一期量產， 較 市 場 預 期
2027~2028 年量產晚， 短 期 獲 利 貢 獻 有 限 ， 且 仍 有 設 備 資 本 支 出 、 折 舊 及 CB 潛在稀
釋 風 險 ， 因 此 短 線 不 宜 追 價 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

台 積 電 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 外 包 布 局 ， 精 材 預 計 2029 年切入 CoWoS 後段 oS 製
程 ：  
精材(3374 TT) 於 11/13/2026 公 告 董 事 會 決 議 通 過 與 具 重 大 影 響 之 投 資 者 台
積電(2330 TT) 簽 訂 產 能 外 包 協 議 ， 契 約 期 間 自 2026/11/13 至 2033/12/31，
雙 方 將 針 對 先 進 封 裝 後 段 製 程 展 開 長 期 策 略 合 作。根 據 公 告 內 容，精 材 計 畫
進駐台積電中部科學園區廠房建置產線並負責生產營運，由台積電提供廠
房空間及先進封裝技術與製造能力支援，精材則自行投入生產設備及自動
物 料 搬 運 系 統 (AMHS)資 本 支 出，切 入 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈 中 的 oS(on-Substrate)
模 組 。 該 產 線 規 劃 於 2029 年 實 現 第 一 期 量 產 ， 雙 方 並 將 共 同 確 保 新 產 線 的
技 術 規 範、品 質 及 良 率 符 合 台 積 電 與 客 戶 要 求。此 外，精 材 董 事 會 同 步 通 過
發 行 新 台 幣 150 億 元 可 轉 換 公 司 債 (CB)， 以 支 應 初 期 產 能 建 置 需 求 。  
既 有 台 積 電 合 作 關 係 延 伸 至 先 進 封 裝 ， 有 助 精 材 拓 展 AI 及 HPC 業務：  
就 精 材 本 身 而 言，此 次 合 作 是 既 有 台 積 電 供 應 鏈 關 係 的 深 化。精 材 原 以 CIS
封 裝 及 晶 圓 級 封 裝 (WLCSP) 為 主 要 業 務，並 承 接 台 積 電 晶 圓 測 試 (CP)外 包 訂
單 ， 惟 既 有 測 試 機 台 於 08/2026 已 達 滿 載 ， 且 受 限 於 無 新 增 無 塵 室 空 間 ， 部
分 台 積 電 測 試 訂 單 轉 由 欣 銓 (3264 TT)承 接。此 次 透 過 進 駐 台 積 電 中 科 廠 區，
精材得以突破自身廠房空間限制，免除購地建廠所需的前置時間與土地成
本，並 利 用 台 積 電 既 有 廠 區 及 技 術 支 援，降 低 新 產 線 建 置 與 製 程 導 入 的 部 分
門 檻。由 於 台 積 電 目 前 持 有 精 材 約 41%股 權，雙 方 已 有 長 期 合 作 基 礎，此 次
進 一 步 由 晶 圓 測 試 及 既 有 封 裝 業 務 延 伸 至 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 ， 有 助 精 材
擴 大 與 台 積 電 的 合 作 範 圍，並 提 高 未 來 AI 及 HPC 相 關 業 務 的 營 收 占 比。不
過，CP 測 試 與 oS 屬 不 同 製 程，仍 須 觀 察 新 產 線 設 備 裝 機、製 程 驗 證 及 良 率
爬 升 進 度 。  
台 積 電 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈 分 工 ， Interposer 與 oS 同 步 建 立 外 部 產 能 ：  
此 次 精 材 切 入 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 ， 應 放 在 台 積 電 持 續 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 供 應
鏈 分 工 的 整 體 策 略 下 觀 察 。 隨 AI 與 HPC 晶片朝 Chiplet、 HBM 及 異 質 整 合
發展，CoWoS 不 同 製 程 環 節 均 面 臨 產 能 擴 充 需 求 。 台 積 電 除 持 續 擴 充 自 身
先 進 封 裝 產 能 外，亦 逐 步 透 過 外 部 合 作 夥 伴 增 加 不 同 環 節 的 供 應 能 力，例 如
Interposer 方 面 與 GlobalFoundries 合 作 建 立 外 部 供 應 來 源，並 透 過 世 界 先 進
等 業 者 擴 充 相 關 產 能；後 段 先 進 封 裝 則 持 續 擴 大 與 OSAT 的 合 作，此 次 再 由
持股約 41%的 精 材 規 劃 於 台 積 電 中 科 廠 區 建 置 oS 產 線 ， 顯 示 台 積 電 正 逐 步
建 立 結 合 關 係 企 業 與 外 部 供 應 商 的 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈，以 提 高 產 能 配 置 彈 性。 
不 同 製 程 環 節 的 外 包 條 件 與 策 略 並 不 相 同 。 Interposer 外 包 主 要 在 於 增 加 供
應 來 源 及 產 能 彈 性；CoWoS 則 可 區 分 為 CoW 與 oS 兩 個 階 段，其 中 CoW 涉
及 晶 圓、矽 中 介 層 及 晶 片 間 的 高 精 度 整 合，製 程 複 雜 度 與 良 率 要 求 較 高，外

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

2 群益投顧 
包 仍 受 技 術 及 良 率 條 件 限 制；相 較 之 下，oS 主 要 負 責 將 完 成 的 CoW 模 組 接
合 至 基 板，隨 OSAT 業 者 製 程 經 驗 累 積 及 良 率 逐 步 成 熟，已 成 為 較 適 合 擴 大
委 外 的 環 節 ， 目 前 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 已 大 量 採 用 外 包 模 式 。 由 於
OSAT 的 先 進 封 裝 毛 利 率 通 常 低 於 台 積 電 整 體 製 造 業 務 ， 將 部 分 oS 產 能 交
由 外 部 業 者 承 接，亦 有 助 台 積 電 將 有 限 的 無 塵 室 空 間、設 備 及 工 程 資 源 優 先
配 置 於 技 術 門 檻 較 高 的 CoW 及 次 世 代 先 進 封 裝 製 程 。  
精材 2029 年 才 量 產 ， 2026～ 2028 年 既 有 OSAT 仍 持 續 受 惠 AI 封 裝 需 求 ：  
精 材 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年 才 開 始 第 一 期 量 產，因 此 在 2026～ 2028 年 間，並 不
會立即改變既有封測業者的供需格局。台積電雖可依製程整合需求向客戶
建 議 合 作 封 測 廠，但 客 戶 仍 可 依 自 身 需 求 直 接 與 封 測 業 者 洽 談 產 能 及 合 作，
因 此 先 進 封 裝 訂 單 並 非 完 全 由 台 積 電 單 方 面 分 配。在 AI/HPC 晶 片 持 續 推 動
先 進 封 裝 規 格 升 級 下，日 月 光 投 控、力 成 及 其 他 既 有 封 測 業 者 仍 可 持 續 受 惠
於新增外包需求，並透過製程改善、良率提升及設備效率優化增加有效產
出。 
至 2029 年 後 ， 精 材 oS 新 產 線 及 其 他 業 者 新 增 產 能 將 陸 續 開 出 ， 加 上 既 有
OSAT 持 續 透 過 良 率 改 善 增 加 有 效 產 能 ， 供 應 鏈 競 爭 可 能 逐 步 加 劇 。 因 此 ，
中 長 期 仍 須 觀 察 AI/HPC 需 求 成 長 能 否 持 續 吸 收 新 增 設 備 及 良 率 提 升 所 帶
來的有效供給，這將是影響先進封裝產業供需及各業者獲利表現的重要變
數。 
2029 年 後 新 增 有 效 產 能 可 能 增 加 ， 精 材 仍 面 臨 資 本 支 出 與 股 本 稀 釋 壓 力 ：  
雖然 AI/HPC 晶 片 持 續 朝 先 進 製 程、Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展，將 支 撐 先 進 封
裝 長 期 需 求 ， 但 目 前 CoWoS 供 不 應 求 ， 除 AI/HPC 需 求 快 速 成 長 、 晶 片 封
裝 面 積 擴 大 及 HBM 整 合 增 加 外 ， 製 程 複 雜 度 高 、 良 率 尚 待 提 升 ， 亦 可 能 限
制 有 效 產 能 釋 出。值 得 留 意 的 是，隨 製 程 技 術 持 續 改 進、設 備 調 校 及 量 產 經
驗 累 積，既 有 產 線 良 率 若 逐 步 提 升，即 使 未 新 增 設 備，也 可 能 增 加 有 效 產 出；
若 再 加 上 台 積 電 及 全 球 OSAT 業 者 持 續 擴 充 產 能 ， 未 來 供 給 增 幅 可 能 高 於
市 場 目 前 預 期 。 因 此 ， 現 階 段 CoWoS 供 不 應 求 未 必 能 長 期 延 續 ， 若 良 率 瓶
頸 逐 步 突 破，供 需 可 能 由 緊 俏 轉 向 平 衡，甚 至 出 現 供 給 較 為 充 裕 的 情 況，後
續 仍 須 觀 察 AI/HPC 需 求 成 長 能 否 吸 收 新 增 設 備 及 良 率 改 善 所 帶 來 的 有 效
供給。  
此 外 ， 精 材 須 自 行 負 擔 設 備 及 AMHS 資 本 支 出 ， 設 備 投 資 至 新 產 線 營 收 貢
獻 仍 有 時 間 落 差，設 備 陸 續 投 入 使 用 後 亦 將 增 加 折 舊 費 用，若 初 期 產 能 利 用
率 及 良 率 爬 升 不 如 預 期，可 能 對 毛 利 率 及 營 業 利 益 形 成 壓 力。資 金 方 面，精
材截至 11/2026 股 本 為 27.14 億 元 ， 約 當 2.714 億 股 ， 預 計 發 行 150 億元
CB， 目 前 尚 未 確 定 轉 換 價 格 ； 若 以 11/13/2026 收 盤 價 519 元 作 為 假 設 轉 換
價格，並以全數轉換估算，新增股數約 2,890 萬 股 ， 相 較 目 前 股 數 增 加 約
10.65%， 轉 換 後 原 股 東 持 股 比 例 將 稀 釋 約 9.63%。 惟 此 為 假 設 情 境 ， 實 際 稀
釋 程 度 仍 須 視 最 終 發 行 金 額、轉 換 價 格 及 轉 換 條 件 而 定。因 此，此 次 合 作 雖
有助精材長期業務結構升級，但仍須考量資本支出回收效益、折舊吸收能
力、CB 潛 在 稀 釋 及 未 來 產 業 供 需 變 化 ， 不 宜 直 接 以 目 前 CoWoS 供 不 應 求
的 狀 況 外 推 未 來 獲 利 。

3 群益投顧 
精 材 短 線 評 價 已 反 映 部 分 成 長 預 期 ， 封 測 股 則 可 留 意 修 正 後 的 布 局 機 會 ：  
精 材 此 次 取 得 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 產 能 外 包 合 作 ， 對 長 期 業 務 結 構 升 級
具 正 面 意 義 ， 惟 股 價 於 公 告 前 已 連 續 6 個 交 易 日 上 漲 ， 累 計 漲 幅 約 40%，
11/13/2026 收 盤 達 519 元 漲 停，本 益 比 已 達 43 倍，加 上 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年
才 開 始 第 一 期 量 產 ， 短 期 獲 利 貢 獻 有 限 ， 且 仍 有 設 備 資 本 支 出 、 折 舊 及 CB
潛 在 稀 釋 風 險 ， 因 此 短 線 不 宜 追 價 。  
另一方面，不宜因精材取得台積電合作，就立即下修日月光投控(3711 TT)
及力成(6239 TT) 的 中 短 期 投 資 看 法 。 2026 年至 2028 年 精 材 新 產 線 尚 未 量
產，既 有 封 測 廠 仍 可 持 續 承 接 AI 及 HPC 先 進 封 裝 與 測 試 外 溢 訂 單，並 透 過
產 線 效 率 及 良 率 改 善 提 升 有 效 產 出，中 短 期 應 以 訂 單 能 見 度、產 能 利 用 率 及
獲 利 表 現 作 為 主 要 觀 察 重 點。4Q26 以 來，部 分 封 測 股 已 因 市 場 對 AI 資 本 支
出 及 投 資 效 益 的 疑 慮 而 大 幅 修 正，若 後 續 訂 單 及 營 運 展 望 未 明 顯 轉 弱，評 價
修正反而可能提供布局機會，可持續留意受惠先進封裝需求的日月光投控
(3711 TT) 、力成(6239 TT)， 以 及 承 接 晶 圓 測 試 外 溢 需 求 的 欣 銓 (3264 TT)。
至 2029 年 後 ， 隨 精 材 及 其 他 業 者 新 增 產 能 陸 續 開 出 ， 加 上 既 有 產 線 良 率 提
升 帶 動 有 效 供 給 增 加 ， 則 須 留 意 供 需 變 化 及 競 爭 加 劇 風 險 。

                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】  
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 ≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <15%

中立(Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等  
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整  
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】  
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為 四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等 係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。   
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」 及 「 區 間 操 作 」 均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。  
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ：  
1. 達 目 標 價 。  
2. 雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。  
3. 推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】 
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 213: 5450亿美元！高盛大幅上调AI项目融资预测，但关键担保人英伟达正在退场

Source PDF: all report pdf/5450亿美元！高盛大幅上调AI项目融资预测，但关键担保人英伟达正在退场.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 4

### Page 1

其中芯⽚融资达 3200 亿美元，较今年增⻓近七倍，其核⼼依赖⼚商担保以对冲折旧⻛险。
然⽽英伟达正收紧担保承诺，其主导的 5000 亿美元融资平台迄今零交易落地。按 25% 担保
⽐例， 3200 亿融资需 800 亿担保⽀撑，⽽三⼤芯⽚商可纳⼊指数债券存量仅 940 亿美元。担
保⼈退场、贷款⽅要求提⾼、买⽅基础收窄，⽆⼈接盘⻛险正上升。
摘要：
⾼盛将 2027 年全球 AI 项⽬融资规模预测从 3000 亿美元⼤幅上调⾄ 5450 亿美元，但这⼀雄⼼勃勃
的预测⾯临⼀个核⼼⽭盾：⽀撑其中最⼤增⻓部分的关键担保⼈ —— 英伟达 —— 正在收缩其⾦
融承诺。
根据⾼盛信贷团队 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇发布的报告， 5450 亿美元预测中有 3200 亿美元来⾃芯⽚融资，这⼀
规模较今年迄今完成的 480 亿美元增⻓近七倍。然⽽，据《华尔街⽇报》报道，英伟达临时 CEO
Colette Kress预计将收紧该公司此前为客⼾提供的担保、回购及投资承诺，⽽英伟达联合创始
⼈⻩仁勋早在 10 ⽉便已告知⼀众新兴云服务商 CEO ，要求其客⼾提前付款。

这⼀转变直接冲击市场信⼼。华尔街⻅闻⽂章此前写道，英伟达今年 8 ⽉联合 Apollo 、⻉莱德、
⿊⽯、 Brookfield 、 KKR 及⾼盛共同发起的 5000 亿美元芯⽚融资平台，迄今零交易落地，原因
是贷款⽅要求英伟达提供超过 25% 的担保覆盖率，⽽英伟达拒绝提⾼⽐例。与此同时，据瑞银
分析师 Tim Arcuri 估算，英伟达哪怕短暂暂停其交易活动，就可能从 AI ⽣态系统中抽⾛ 500 亿美
元以上的资⾦。
从 3000 亿到 5450 亿：预测⼤幅上调背后的逻辑
⾼盛信贷策略团队在最新报告《 The Credit Line: From Shell to Compute: An Update on AI
Project Finance 》中披露， 2026 年迄今全球 AI 项⽬融资总规模已达 1500 亿美元，其中投资级与
⾼收益数据中⼼债券 800 亿美元、⾮银团建设融资 220 亿美元、芯⽚融资 480 亿美元。这⼀数字
已远超该团队年初预测的 760 亿美元全年规模。

⾼盛将 5450 亿美元预测拆分为两部分：数据中⼼ “ 外壳 ” 融资 2250 亿美元，芯⽚（即 GPU 及定制
芯⽚）融资 3200 亿美元。前者较今年规模约翻倍，后者则增⻓近七倍。
⽅法论上，⾼盛以计划投⼊使⽤的数据中⼼容量为基础，假设美国每兆⽡外壳成本约 1500 万美
元，内部芯⽚成本约 2500 万美元，融资⽐例⽅⾯外壳为 45% ⾄ 50% ，超⼤规模云服务商
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

（ hyperscaler ）的算⼒项⽬为 30% ，其他项⽬为 45% 。芯⽚每兆⽡成本约为外壳的 1.7 倍，这意
味着⼀旦贷款⽅开始为内部设备融资，数字将急速膨胀。
⾼盛股票分析师预计 2028 年前将有 113 吉⽡算⼒出货，但信贷团队仅能找到 80 吉⽡的数据中⼼
容量与之匹配。按每兆⽡ 2500 万美元计算， 113 吉⽡对应约 2.8 万亿美元的芯⽚采购需求，其中
约三分之⼀尚⽆明确的安置场所。
芯⽚融资的运作机制：担保是核⼼
芯⽚融资在结构上与数据中⼼债券类似：⼀个破产隔离的特殊⽬的载体（ SPV ）持有 GPU 资
产，客⼾签署租约或服务合同，贷款⽅从合同现⾦流中获得偿还。⾼盛将现有交易分为两类：
设备租赁（类似建筑物的三净租约，租⼾直接⽀付芯⽚租⾦）和算⼒服务合同（ SPV 出售算
⼒，收⼊取决于服务实际运⾏情况）。
抵押品的快速折旧是核⼼⻛险。⾼盛在报告中明确建议：
正因如此，芯⽚制造商的剩余价值担保（ residual value guarantee ）成为整个结构的关键。在

这⼀机制下，若合同付款中断且芯⽚价值低于承诺⾦额，担保⽅负责补⾜差额。⾼盛指出，在
⼤多数交易中，担保⾦额接近剩余本⾦与利息之和 —— 换⾔之，贷款⽅实际上是在对芯⽚制造
商进⾏信⽤评估，⽽芯⽚制造商则在为⾃⼰的客⼾背书。
即便有担保⽀撑，投资级芯⽚融资的新发利差仍⾼达 150 ⾄ 240 个基点，⽽普通 5 ⾄ 7 年期投资级
债券约为 100 个基点；杠杆融资领域最新成交利差接近 775 个基点，约为同评级 BB/B 信⽤的三
倍。
英伟达退场： 3200 亿美元融资缺⼝谁来填补
⾼盛报告虽⻓达 13 ⻚，但 “ 英伟达（ Nvidia ） ” ⼀词仅在⼀张图表的脚注中出现⼀次。然⽽，英伟
达的⻆⾊恰恰是整个预测最关键的变量。
据《华尔街⽇报》报道， Kress 预计将收紧英伟达通过担保、租约、回购和初创投资为客⼾提供
融资的各类窗⼝。⾼盛报告显⽰，英伟达截⾄ 7 ⽉底的承诺与担保总额达 5300 亿美元，其中包括
为 OpenAI 俄亥俄州租约提供的 1050 亿美元背书。
英伟达 8 ⽉发起的 5000 亿美元融资平台迄今零交易落地，原因在于贷款⽅要求的担保覆盖率⾼于

英伟达愿意提供的 25% 上限。相⽐之下，博通（ Broadcom ）在今年 5 ⽉为 Anthropic 的 TPU 项⽬
提供了 350 亿美元中约 72.5% 的担保覆盖，并在⼀笔 630 亿美元的交易中提供了类似⽐例的⽀
持。
据路透社报道，多位市场⼈⼠对此表态：摩根⼤通的 Erica Spear 预计现有承诺将维持，但新交
易将需要更⻓时间或更⾼补偿；摩根⼠丹利的 Joseph Moore 认为 5000 亿美元第三⽅资本更可能
流向超⼤规模云服务商等成熟参与者； PGIM 的 Edwin Wilches 表⽰，贷款⽅希望未来融资以客
⼾合同或⾦融担保为⽀撑，⽽⾮以芯⽚本⾝作为抵押加有限担保；美国银⾏估计，截⾄今年 8 ⽉
英伟达已提供约 2300 亿美元的⾦融担保。
“ 信贷投资者应尽量避免承担折旧和潜在过时⻛险，传统优先留置权融资通常不适合为这类快速
折旧、可能过时的资产提供如此规模的融资。 ”

### Page 3

按照 25% 的担保⽐例， 3200 亿美元的芯⽚融资需要 800 亿美元担保；按博通的条款，则需要
2300 亿⾄ 2750 亿美元。⽽英伟达、博通和 AMD 三家芯⽚制造商合计仅有 940 亿美元的可纳⼊指
数债券存量。
信⽤市场的溢出效应：冲击曲线中段
芯⽚租赁期限通常在五年以内，这意味着相关融资将集中落在收益率曲线 3 ⾄ 7 年期区间。⾼盛
数据显⽰，过去两年该区间占所有⾮⾦融投资级发⾏⼈的 60% 、融资⾦额的 40% ，是企业债市
场最核⼼的借贷区间。
⾼盛认为，短期内冲击将保持可控，原因在于：结构化交易不纳⼊主流指数，分析难度较⾼，
买⽅基础相对狭窄。但这同时意味着，3200 亿美元芯⽚债务的潜在买⽅将排除指数基⾦、不具
备结构化信贷分析能⼒的机构，以及已对相关租⼾持仓过重的投资者。
在利差层⾯，英伟达等芯⽚制造商的 CDS 相对其现⾦债券的溢价，已从⼀年前的负值升⾄约 75
个基点，⾼于超⼤规模云服务商约 45 个基点的⽔平。⾼盛预计，随着芯⽚制造商信⽤敞⼝持续
超越其债券规模，这⼀差距将继续扩⼤。

数据中⼼ “ 外壳 ” 融资同样承压
⾼盛预测中的 2250 亿美元外壳融资部分同样⾯临挑战。⾼盛追踪的 16 只⾼收益数据中⼼债券
中，共同基⾦和 ETF 对其中 13 只持有低配，平均主动权重为 -0.07% 。投资级数据中⼼交易则未
被纳⼊美国综合指数和美国企业债指数，主要买⽅为允许偏离基准的资产管理⼈（平均持有每
笔交易约四分之⼀）和保险公司（约 10% ）。
融资成本的上升也在压缩项⽬可⾏性。今年上半年新发数据中⼼债券收益率为 6% ⾄ 8% ，近期⾼
收益交易已升⾄ 8% ⾄ 10% 。⾼盛测算，在 90% 贷款成本⽐、 1.2 倍偿债覆盖率的假设下，票息从
8% 升⾄ 9% ，租⼾需多⽀付 1.4 个百分点的租⾦收益率以维持覆盖率；若票息升⾄ 10% ，则需多
⽀付 2.7 个百分点。
⾼盛的结论是：若票息持续⾛⾼，项⽬要么需要更多股权、更低杠杆和更严格结构，要么租⾦
必须相应上调 —— 这两种结果都意味着每个项⽬的债务规模将低于预期。
三条出路，⼀个未知数
⾼盛的预测本质上是对融资需求的判断，⽽⾮对融资能否实现的保证。报告本⾝建议投资者 “ 保

持耐⼼和选择性 ” ，并指出 “ 宏观和技术⾯的⻉塔效应更可能推动利差⾛阔，⽽⾮收窄 ” 。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 362: How Polish households are saving and investing in 2026

Source PDF: all report pdf/How Polish households are saving and investing in 2026.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Why Poland’s new Personal Investment Account could prove
popular with Polish investors
Leszek Kasek
Poles remain cautious investors, with a preference for cash and bank deposits
Our recent survey paints a positive picture of Polish households’
ﬁnances. A large share have savings, many are open to investing in
capital markets for the ﬁrst time, and welcome regulatory and tax
incentives.
Against this backdrop, the new Personal Investment Account
scheme (OKI), due to come into force in 2027, could ﬁnd fertile

ground. It addresses the knowledge gap, encourages regular saving
and investing, and oﬀers tax relief. The scheme could help Poles
shift from conservative savers towards more informed investors.
An IPSOS survey commissioned by ING Bank Śląski on Poles’
attitudes towards saving and investing (a sample of 1,001
respondents), compared with selected European countries
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.

Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

(including Germany, Spain, the Netherlands and Romania), found
that Poles:
View their ﬁnancial situation more optimistically, although
they are not among the wealthiest in Europe.
Are more likely to report having savings, although these are
relatively lower than in other countries.
Prefer liquidity (cash and bank deposits) to the potential returns
oﬀered by other ﬁnancial instruments, indicating greater risk
aversion.
These conservative attitudes are borne out by ‘hard’ National

Bank of Poland data on Polish households’ ﬁnancial assets.
Are also more likely than other Europeans to describe investing
in ﬁnancial instruments as being as risky as gambling in a
casino.
Prefer to invest in their own country.
Are more likely to regard property and gold as good investment
alternatives.
Often associate saving with concerns about retirement.
Like other Europeans, they say they lack knowledge about
investing and view schemes supporting saving and investment

through government regulation and tax incentives more
favourably.
This could provide a promising environment for the Open
Investment Account scheme (OKI) in 2027. By combining tax
incentives with measures to improve ﬁnancial knowledge and
encourage regular investing, the scheme could help shift Polish
households away from a traditionally conservative approach
towards more active and informed investment.
About the survey: The survey of individuals’ saving and investment

preferences covered 11 European countries (Belgium, France, Spain,
the Netherlands, Germany, Poland, Romania, Switzerland, Türkiye,
the United Kingdom and Italy) and Australia. Samples comprised
around 1,000 people in each country and were representative in
terms of sex, age, marital status, education, labour market activity,
household size and monthly income.

### Page 3

Key ﬁndings from comparing Poland with
other European countries
In this article, we compare the survey results for Poland with the
average for European countries (Europe), as well as the results for
the Netherlands and Germany (countries wealthier than Poland),
Spain (a country similar to Poland in terms of population and GDP
per capita) and Romania (a country with lower GDP per capita than
Poland).
Poles feel better off, despite lagging
European wealth levels

75% of Polish respondents believe they are living comfortably
or doing quite well, compared with a European average of 55%
and just under 50% in Romania.
Poles assess their ﬁnancial situation optimistically, although in
2025 their stock of net ﬁnancial assets (assets minus liabilities)
relative to GDP (78% of GDP) was less than half the EU27
average (165% of GDP). Compared with 2017, net assets
increased by around 13% of GDP, but this was driven by a

decline in liabilities (deleveraging), while the stock of ﬁnancial
assets remained stable (at around 100% of GDP).
According to Eurostat data on the composition of gross
ﬁnancial assets, around half of Poles’ assets (49%, compared
with 30% in the EU27) consisted of cash and bank deposits
(current and term deposits) in 2025. The share of cash
increased markedly after the pandemic (from 9% of ﬁnancial
assets in 2017 to 11% in 2025) and was the highest among

the countries under review. Compared with wealthier European
countries, Poland stood out for the signiﬁcant share of loans
(5% of the total), most of which comprised retail Treasury
savings bonds. In EU statistics, these instruments are treated as
loans to the public sector rather than debt securities because of
their limited transferability (only bonds listed on the Warsaw
Stock Exchange are included in the latter category). Compared
with the EU27, Poland has a lower share of shares and other

equity in total ﬁnancial assets (19% versus 27%), as well as
investment fund shares or units (6% versus 11%), and
particularly a lower share of assets in insurance and pension
schemes (14% versus 26%).

Source: IPSOS survey
As many as 77% of Poles say that their household has savings,
a ﬁgure second only to the Netherlands (88%) and above the
European average (74%).
However, Poles’ savings are not substantial. 54% of
respondents report savings equivalent to no more than six
months’ net income, while wealthier Europeans have
substantially larger savings, exceeding six months’ net income.
Poles are more likely than other Europeans
to cite higher living costs as a reason for

having no savings
Source: IPSOS survey
Among respondents who report having no savings, around
60% across all countries say that they do not earn enough.
Among other factors, a relatively high proportion in Poland
(16%) cite higher living costs (compared with a European
average of 14%).

### Page 6

Compared with other countries, relatively many Poles tend to
agree that their household has too little savings to invest.
Poles favour liquidity over higher returns,
indicating greater risk aversion
Source: IPSOS survey
Around 40% of Poles say they prefer to keep their savings liquid
so that they can spend them easily if needed. This attitude
does not diﬀer substantially from the European average; the
proportions in Germany and the Netherlands are actually

signiﬁcantly higher.
Almost 70% say they do not invest in capital market
instruments such as shares, bonds, investment funds or ETFs.
This proportion is slightly higher than the European average,
but lower than in the Netherlands. Among those who said they
did not currently invest, however, Poland has the highest
proportion of respondents open to taking this step in future
(29%) (I have not invested, but I may do so one day), compared
with 24% in Europe. This indicates Poles’ openness to new

forms of investing.
Poles’ views on long-term returns reﬂect
investment caution
More than half of Poles (55%) regard gold and property as the
most proﬁtable investments, compared with a European
average of 46%. (These categories are not classiﬁed as
ﬁnancial assets in Eurostat statistics).

► 美股自上週的低點反彈，上週跌幅最深的費
半反彈幅度最大，反映短線市場恐慌情緒有
所緩解。然而，AI疑慮並未完全消散，儘管
德州州長當選人Gina Hinojosa表示並非全面
反對資料中心開發，但資料中心監管趨嚴的
疑慮仍在市場迴盪。另一方面，10月CPI數
據高於預期，搭配優於預期的非農數據，支
撐了市場對於聯準會12月升息1碼之預估，

加上輝達執行長黃仁勳的相關事件，皆成為
短線股市反彈能否延續所需觀察的變數。
► 產業方面，CoreWeave財報與展望優於市場
預期，並進一步說明積壓訂單、客戶承諾與
融資規畫，帶動半導體、伺服器、記憶體及
資料中心相關個股買盤。然而，輝達則受執
行長 黃 仁 勳遭 到 調 查且 宣 布 請辭 之 消 息影
響， 股 價 因而 回 落 並抵 銷 科 技類 股 部 分漲
勢。其餘產業則是呈現上漲，但醫療保健、
必需消費等防禦型產業在恐慌情緒放緩下多
數漲幅落後於成長型產業。

► 美國10月CPI與核心CPI月增率均高於市場預
期，推升市場對Fed進一步升息的預估。加
上Fed理事Waller表示，若核心CPI月增率維
持0.3%或更高，12月應考慮升息兩碼，相關
消息同步推升美國公債殖利率，並帶動整體
債券市場跌多漲少。
► 原先市場擔憂期中選舉後川普將再度攻擊伊
朗，然隨近期川普一度淡化對伊朗發動軍事
攻擊的可能性，並稱戰爭已實質結束，市場
因而下修中東地緣政治溢價，並帶動布蘭特
油價大幅回落至每桶90美元附近。惟川普於
週間再度指稱不需與伊朗達成協議，短時間
態度反覆變化令市場對美伊談判疑慮再起，
預料油價短期將呈現區間震盪。
► 受市場強化聯準會升息預期支撐，帶動美元
指數呈現強勁上漲，並同步拖累歐元與日圓
顯著走弱，其中美元兌日圓匯價已逼近160
之整數關卡，短期恐引發干預措施的預期，
走勢料偏向震盪。

► 受能源價格增速放緩影響，美國10月CPI月
增率由前值0.6%降至0.4%，核心CPI月增率
維持0.3%水準，惟整體表現高於預期，其中
食品、住房及運輸服務持續上漲，顯示核心
通膨相 對具 有黏 性 ，物價 壓力未 見全 面緩
解；此外，因去年聯邦政府關門造成統計缺
漏，故本次不公布年增率數值。考量美國就
業市場表現強勁，通膨降溫進程不如預期，
疊加近期聯準會官員偏鷹發言，強化市場押
注年底升息一碼之預期。
► 隨民主黨拿下參眾兩院，美伊重啟和談訊號
浮現，市場對戰事降溫抱持正向期待，帶動
國際油價一度自高檔回落；此外，受2026年
油價高基期影響，若油價未再進一步出現大
幅攀升，預估2027年原油價格年增速可望趨

於收斂，對於整體通膨的推升力道亦有望減
緩。美國方面，受惠於頁岩油生產效率提升
及墨西哥灣新離岸油田陸續投產，則可望帶
動美國原油產量持續成長。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 近期美股市場風格加速轉換，隨著市場更加
重視AI商業化成效與投資回報，高估值成長
股波動顯著升溫。相較之下，價值股則逐漸
受到市場青睞，尤其是憑藉穩定現金流、健
康資產負債表與較高股東回報率之高品質價
值股，除了擁有價值優勢之外，更具備更好
防禦力與抗震能力。
► 從風險維度進行觀察，近10年股市面臨3次
升息循環，不論是2016年、2022年甚至是
2026年9月啟動之升息，高品質價值股指數
之90天波動度平均值皆低於成長股與大盤指
數，顯示若將具備穩定獲利、自由現金流與
較低財 務槓 桿之 高 品質價 值股納 入投 資組
合，有助降低市場修正期間的波動風險。
► 長期而言，高品質價值股的累積報酬率雖不
及成長股在景氣擴張期的爆發力，但仍具優
於大盤的累積報酬潛力，代表在市場波動加
劇、利率升高的環境下，其兼具防禦與長期
複利之優勢已然是長期投資不可或缺關鍵。
高品質價值股長期報酬優於大盤價值股相對成長股獲市場青睞，波動度亦相對偏低

### Page 7

7
投資焦點國防股
► 國防股通常具備穩定攀升之自由現金流，主
要受惠於長期政府合約與產業高進入門檻所
支持。相較景氣循環股，國防產業獲利與現
金流波動幅度較低，使其兼具一定程度的防
禦性與長期價值配置優勢。
► 北美國防企業近年受惠於地緣政治風險升溫
與軍備補庫存需求，訂單能見度明顯提升。
包含飛彈、防空系統、軍機及潛艦等關鍵裝
備，有助維持歷史高檔積壓訂單水位。龐大
訂單庫存不僅支撐未來數年營收成長，也有
助降低短期景氣波動對企業基本面的影響。
► 根據SIPRI 資料，2025 年 全 球 軍 事 支 出 達
2.89兆美元，已連續11年成長，全球軍事支
出占GDP比重升至2.5%，創2009年以來新
高。其中歐洲軍費年增14%、亞洲與大洋洲
年增 8.1%。在美伊衝突、區域安全風險及北
約提高國防支出目標等因素帶動下，各國軍
事現代化需求可望延續，皆將為國防產業提
供長期且具結構性的成長動能。
地緣政治問題未解，全球軍事支出持續增加國防產業現金流穩定攀升，積壓訂單成長強勁
現金流穩定，補庫存預期支撐後續國防股走勢

### Page 8

8
股市
 黃仁勳相關事件恐逐步強化美國對AI晶片出口管制、AI資料中心建設進程或將受到州政府緊縮政策
而造成評價面壓力並增添股價波動。短期可留意防禦股以增強抗震力以抵禦市場震盪。長期而言，
AI從全面看好轉向選股，且大優於小，市場波瀾過後產業長期正向趨勢未變。當前則可布局財務健
全度較高的高品質價值股，以獲取穩定報酬並降低市場波動影響。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好轉向選股、且大優於小，市場波
瀾後長期正向趨勢未變，當前可布局財務
健全度較高的高品質價值股
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 美伊談判走向反覆未明，油價維持高檔震盪。10月CPI高於預期，強化市場對聯準會升息步伐預估，
聯準會官員立場亦偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率
走升時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具
吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。

債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 10月CPI數據支撐升息預期，美元走勢偏強。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理
商品
 美伊談判走向反覆，油價一度回落但再度反彈，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

## Document 391: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-18)

Source PDF: all report pdf/New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-18).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.

New Zealand 2026 General Election:

Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
The result.
The preliminary results of the 2026 General Election,
based on the election night count, are set out in the table
above. In summary:
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First and ACT
won 67 seats.
• The centre-left bloc of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti
Māori won 57 seats.
• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling and will
enter parliament with 7 seats.
These results indicate a swing away from National to its

coalition partners while the centre-left grouping picked
up a few seats. The big gainer was the Opportunity Party
who perhaps took a big share of what otherwise could
have been a larger swing to the centre-left grouping.
The size of parliament will expand by around nine seats,
as National held onto a large share of its electorate
votes and hence has more seats than it would have been
entitled to based on its party vote share. As a result, a
governing bloc would require 66 seats to command a

majority in parliament.
Several of the electorate seats are very tight (see Table
2), so election night majorities could easily be overturned
once special votes are counted. Of particular relevance
is the seat of Wairarapa, where the National candidate is
leading the Labour candidate by just 139 votes. If Labour
were to win that seat (specials favoured Labour in 2020
but National in 2023), all else equal the centre-right seat
count would decline to 66 seats in what would become

a 130-seat Parliament – just enough to command a
majority. But if either NZ First or ACT were to lose a single
party vote seat to the left bloc once specials are counted
then that majority would disappear.
On that score, historically the election night results have
tended to overstate the support of the centre-right bloc
and understate the support for parties on the left of
the political spectrum. For example, over the previous
three elections (2017, 2020, 2023), the current centre-

right bloc’s vote share has declined 1.9%, 1.7% and 1.6%
respectively following the counting of special votes, with
the National Party losing 2 seats on each occasion once
the official results were declared (Labour, the Greens and
Te Pāti Māori have benefited). On this occasion, given its
overhang, a lower party vote share would not lower the
National Party’s final vote count, although it could alter
the size of its overhang and thus the number of seats

required to command a majority. In recent elections both
NZ First and ACT have seen their vote shares decline
following the counting of special votes.
The process from here.
Official results.
The official results will be declared on 27 November,
adding the results of an estimated 400,000 special votes
(with around a quarter of these cast overseas) to the
2.41m votes counted on election night.
We expect discussions between potential coalition
partners will begin immediately to start sketching out the

parameters of a coalition agreement. But given the tight
margins of the current vote count, no firm positions will
be agreed until the results are declared.
The writ and recounts.
After 27 November, the writ is scheduled to be returned
on 3 December – formally certifying who has been
elected. However, that date is subject to any recounts

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
that might be requested. Parliament is required to meet
no later than 14 January (six weeks after the return of the
writ), providing an opportunity for a new government
to test that it has the confidence of the House. It is
worth noting that Parliament can be summoned and
begin functioning even if coalition negotiations are still
occurring. If no coalition or confidence-and-supply
arrangement has been reached by then, negotiations can

simply continue and the incumbent Government remains
in office under the caretaker convention (Ministers in
the outgoing government retain executive authority
temporarily, even if they are no longer MPs).
Given that the Pre-Election Economic and Fiscal
Update was released in late September there is no legal
requirement for the Treasury to publish a Half Year
Economic and Fiscal Update (HYEFU) this year. And given
that it will be some weeks before a government is formed,

it seems unlikely that there will be a government in place
that would request that a HYEFU be produced despite
there being no statutory requirement (certainly there
would be little time to incorporate policy changes that
fall out of new coalition agreements, thus diminishing
its value). Once a government is formed, it will have an
opportunity to lay out its immediate priorities in the
Budget Policy Statement (BPS), which by law needs to
be released before the end of March next year. The new

government could decide to release an interim fiscal
update with the BPS if that was thought necessary.
Potential coalition outcomes.
We see three main likely outcomes:
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First.
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First with the support of the Opportunity Party
on confidence and supply.
• Some coalition of Labour, the Greens, Opportunity and
Te Pāti Māori, potentially with Opportunity and Te Pāti

Māori sitting outside the cabinet.
Our preview of the election discussed the policies of
the main political parties, while the figure on page 5
highlights some points of commonality with the centre-
right and centre-left blocs.
National, ACT and NZ First share a broadly pro-
development and deregulatory agenda, including support
for greater resource development, fewer regulatory
barriers and compulsory or expanded KiwiSaver. Beyond
that common core, National and ACT align particularly

closely on lower taxes and deregulation, while National
and NZ First share an emphasis on infrastructure, regional
development and law and order. ACT and NZ First favour
tighter immigration settings and tighter welfare policies.
The differences are clearest at the edges: ACT advocates
deeper tax cuts, smaller government and more market-
led provision, while NZ First places greater emphasis
on economic sovereignty, regional development and
targeted support for working New Zealanders. National

occupies more of the middle ground, combining fiscal
discipline and a pro-business agenda with increased
infrastructure and housing supply.
The Opportunity Party presents itself as a middle
alternative that can work with the left or the right
groupings. They have five bottom line policies they say
they need to support any new government. These include
competition reform through enhanced Commerce
Commission powers, more restrictions on political
lobbyists and donors, a ten-year plan of investment in the

healthcare sector, an end to bottom trawling in inshore
areas and a phase out elsewhere and for the Government
to recommit to New Zealand’s Paris Agreement
obligations. Their success in meeting these bottom
lines with a new government and their flexibility around
these requirements will be key to their ability to form an
agreement with either side. There is better congruence
of the Opportunity Party’s policies with the left than the
right – so progress here will be critical to watch should

their support be required to form a new government. We
suspect they will be very motivated to avoid the need for
a new election via a hung parliament should push come
to shove.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Labour, the Greens and Opportunity share support
for increased public and social investment, a broader
tax base, greater infrastructure investment, stronger
competition policy, cheaper public transport and a
more active role for government. Labour and the Greens
overlap particularly on higher taxation, public investment
and stronger climate and environmental policy, while
Labour and Opportunity share an emphasis on social

investment and higher public-service wages. The Greens
and Opportunity both favour broader taxation and
stronger climate action, although their preferred tax
instruments differ. Their distinctive priorities are also
clear: Labour places more weight on worker protections
and restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate; the Greens on
environmental protection, progressive taxation and Te
Tiriti; and Opportunity on land-value taxation, housing
infrastructure, productivity, innovation and evidence-

based policy.

and differences.

## Document 447: US MARKETS CALL_ Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea

Source PDF: all report pdf/US MARKETS CALL_ Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US MARKETS CALL: Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea
Date:    2 hours ago
US MARKETS CALL: Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US MARKETS CALL: Below The
Calm Deep Blue Sea
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 14 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Thomas Vimare / Unsplash

The major stock market indexes have gone nowhere since early August.
On the surface, the seas have been calm. Underneath, plenty of
turbulence has been occurring. Most of the volatility has been within
sectors.
In mid-September, we warned our readers to proceed with caution through
the midterm elections. We expected and are now seeing buying

### Page 2

opportunities. The S&P 500 closed at 7,561.67 on Friday. It has been
fluctuating around 7,600 since August 3. We think it is set for another rally
that should take it up to 7,900 by the end of this year.
I. Stock Market Performance
The S&P 500 is down just 0.5% since August 3, while the equal-weight
version of the index is up 1.8% (chart). The MAGS ETF is up 0.4%, while
the XMAGS ETF is up 1.2%. The Nasdaq is down 3.0%. Only the Russell
2000 has moved much, falling 6.4%.

The sectors underneath the S&P 500 have been choppier (chart). Health
Care has gained 16.4% since August 3, and Consumer Staples is up
10.8%. Communication Services is down 11.3%, and Industrials is down
7.9%. Information Technology, at the center of the AI selloff, is down 3.5%.

### Page 3

The Information Technology sector has also been extremely turbulent
below the surface (chart). The Semiconductor Equipment and
Semiconductors industries are down 23.9% and 13.6% since August 3.
Meanwhile, IT Consulting is up 22.3%. Application Software and Systems
Software are up 12.0% and 4.5%. The crosscurrents have largely offset
each other.
The Magnificent-7 stocks have diverged too (chart). Nvidia’s market cap
has dropped to $3.92 trillion following news of CEO Jensen Huang’s

resignation. Apple’s market cap is at a record $5.18 trillion. Alphabet’s fell
after reporting earnings but has since stabilized at $3.62 trillion.
II. Stock Market Earnings

### Page 4

Industry analysts are usually too optimistic about the earnings of the
companies they follow. As quarterly earnings reporting seasons approach,
they are often forced to cut their estimates. That's evident in their annual
consensus "Earnings Squiggles," which tend to start high and then fall to
the reported results (chart). This time, the annual Earnings Squiggles for
2026, 2027, and 2028 all are rising. That’s highly unusual. It might reflect
some irrational exuberance among analysts. Then again, they've gotten

more optimistic as actual earnings have come in stronger than anticipated
in recent quarters.
The recent market turbulence hasn’t dented S&P 500 operating earnings-
per-share estimates. The 2027 consensus rose to $427.01 last week, and
the 2028 consensus rose to $496.24 (chart). Both are new highs. Forward
earnings hit another record high at $420.15 per share, edging closer to the
2027 estimate. We are estimating $425 for next year.

### Page 5

Q3’s consensus earnings growth estimate rose again, to 26.9% y/y from
25.9% a week earlier (chart). Q4’s estimate edged up to 29.8%. The Q2
actual earnings growth soared on mark-to-market accounting gains.
Without them, earnings rose about 25% rather than 50.4%.
III. Stock Market Valuation
Valuation multiples continue to fall. The S&P 500’s forward P/E is down to
18.0, while the Magnificent-7’s is down to 21.4 (chart). We think the
contraction may be close to a bottom. Our year-end S&P 500 target

implies a forward P/E of 18.6. Multiples usually shrink on recession fears.
We don’t see a recession coming. Expectations of a slowdown in the AI
buildout may be weighing on valuations. In our view, the slowdown
constitutes speed bumps, not roadblocks.

### Page 6

Only 36.1% of S&P 500 stocks now trade above a forward P/E of 20,
down from nearly 50% earlier this year (chart). That’s a healthy
development, driven by the air coming out of the AI bubble, which is now
less likely to burst.
The multiple correction has been centered in S&P 500 Growth, not Value,
stocks. The S&P 500 Growth forward P/E has dropped 27% to 19.0 from
26.0 at the start of this year, while Value is at 18.0, about the same as at
the beginning of the year (chart).

### Page 7

The spread between the Growth and Value forward P/Es has narrowed to
just 1.0 point, the narrowest since 2010 (chart). Investors aren’t paying up
for growth anymore.
Only three sectors have gotten more expensive this year: Real Estate,
Consumer Staples, and Health Care (chart). Information Technology’s
forward P/E has dropped from 26.5 at the end of last year to 17.2.
Communication Services’ forward P/E is down from 22.1 to 17.0.

### Page 8

IV. Credit
The 10-year Treasury bond yield jumped to 5.10% after October's CPI and
PPI reports (chart). That’s still below its October peak above 5.30%.
The 10-year yield is hovering near the top end of the 4.00%-5.00% range
we consider normal (chart).

### Page 9

The latest increase in the 10-year nominal yield has been attributable to
the 10-year TIPS yield. The spread between the two, which is a proxy for
10-year expected inflation, remains low at 2.21% (chart). That's surprising
since the recent yield rise followed the hotter-than-expected CPI and PPI
reports.
Credit default swap spreads rose in recent months on concerns about the
debt of CoreWeave and other AI borrowers but have since narrowed
(chart). We don’t see broadening credit stress.

### Page 10

V. Commodities
Commodity prices are signaling less inflationary pressure. The FIBER
Commodities Spot Price Index is looking toppy (chart).
The S&P GSCI Energy Spot Index also seems to be topping out along
with oil prices (chart).

### Page 11

Even diesel prices have come down substantially. The Gulf Coast spot
price fell to $4.11 a gallon from a peak above $5.00 earlier this year
(chart).
Agricultural prices have also eased from their summer highs (chart).

### Page 12

The copper price has been getting hit along with the AI trade. It has fallen
to $6.08 a pound from its recent record high above $6.80 (chart).
💡
Join the discussion with Ed below! To leave comments or questions,
log in to the Yardeni QuickTakes website and post them at the end
of the QuickTakes article. Paid members' contributions may be
featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

價相對不利。
近期隨著部分AI與經濟敏感型股票面臨估值修正與
財報疑慮，市場資金開始撤出高基期板塊，轉向醫

型領域，但在目前利率仍高的情況下，預估短期內

公用事業走勢將呈區間內來回震盪。

零售客流量表
重點摘要
• 美國多州出現暫停資料中心建
設的政策訴求，資金可能加速
流向規則較為明確的州。
• 電力股的投資邏輯正在轉向，
未來將更看重獲利品質與監管
可預測性。
• AI資料中心電力需求仍是公用
事業3Q26財報重點，但焦點轉
向如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成
本。
《部分電力股評價將更看重監管可預測性》
在2026年美國期中選舉涉及的36場州長選戰中，已
有20州出現暫停資料中心建設或許可的政策訴求。
電價、用水與基礎設施成本的分攤爭議，正改寫各
州爭取AI投資的方向，資金可能從監管混沌的地區，
加速流向規則較為明確的州。德州為這輪監管分化
中最值得關注、也最棘手的地區。德州電網接入申
請佇列已成長到約474吉瓦，其中約九成來自資料

中心，而德州州長已宣布暫停核准新的資料中心接
入州內電網，9月底環境委員會的暫停令進一步擴及
自備電源的離網專案，意味企業即使自己發電，也
未必能繞過許可關卡。
相較之下，在大規模用電需求湧入前，先把監管制
度架好的州或許更具產業前景。懷俄明、明尼蘇達、
愛荷華、威斯康辛、內華達、南卡羅來納與田納西
州為潛在的資金承接者。明尼蘇達已立法防止大客
戶把成本轉嫁給一般用戶，科羅拉多的大負荷費率
方案則要求至少15年合約、80%最低計費需求與提
前退出費，把部分退出風險交還給客戶。電力股的
投資邏輯正在轉向，未來部分公用事業股的評價分
化，將從過去單純看需求規模，轉向更看重獲利品
質與監管可預測性。
《AI資料中心電力需求仍為近期財報重點，但焦點
轉向如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成本》
隨著3Q26財報陸續披露，美國公用事業公司對AI資
料中心用電需求普遍釋出極為樂觀且迫切的長線成
長展望。以FirstEnergy為例，受惠於毗鄰北弗吉尼
亞數據中心走廊的優勢，西弗吉尼亞州等地的資料
中 心 相 關 負 載 需 求 激 增 137% 達到4.3 吉瓦。

American Electric Power其累積的增量負載承諾已
飆升至56吉瓦，半年內暴增一倍。但公用事業高層
的態度在積極擴產中也帶有審慎風險控管，為了避
免新建發電廠及電網升級的費用侵蝕一般用戶權益，
業者正重新設計合約，要求科技公司簽署最低電費
與信用擔保條款。整體而言，公用事業財報反映出
資料中心用電需求並未降溫，但產業焦點已從「如
何接到訂單」轉向「如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成本」
的階段。

## Document 485: 【產業聚焦】降息前的美國汽車產業投資布局

Source PDF: all report pdf/【產業聚焦】降息前的美國汽車產業投資布局.pdf
Report source: CTBC
Date: 2026-10-30

### Page 1

源，但對其完整性、即時性和正確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資

產業聚焦降息前的美國汽車產業投資布局
2026/10/30
• 美國傳統車商迎來皮卡改款
週期
• 高油價使油電、電動車需求
提升
• 自駕車、軟體營收增加帶動
車商獲利
美國傳統車商迎來皮卡改款週期，獲利表現有望提升
美國傳統車廠近年轉向生產更多高價卡車和SUV，因部分SUV和皮卡的調整後息稅前利潤率高達20%、超
過其他車型，也使美國新車平均交易價格在9月超過5萬美元。福特與通用汽車即將在2026年年底至2027
年迎來全尺寸皮卡改款與更新計畫，由於在推出新款或新一代卡車後，通常會有2~3年較佳的銷售期，預
估福特和通用汽車在2027年及之後的獲利有機會持續提升。
美國政策轉為支持燃油車發展，但高油價使油電、電動車需求提升

儘管美國政策轉向支持燃油車發展，但自美伊戰爭後油價居高不下，使油電混合車3Q26在美國的市佔率
暴衝至16%，包括豐田、現代等品牌在美國的市占率持續上升。美國3Q26電動車銷量約佔新車總銷量
6.5%，高於2Q26的5.8%，顯示電動車在美國市場正重新站穩腳步。由於油價上升使全球對電動車需求
增加，特斯拉2Q26、3Q26交車量也優於市場預期。
自駕車接受度提升，軟體營收增加帶動車商獲利
美國消費者對自動駕駛的認識與接受度正持續提升，而各車商正積極擴大自駕與軟體訂閱服務布局。通用
汽車預估其OnStar和Super Cruise自駕輔助營收在2026年將突破30億美元，福特汽車的軟體付費訂閱用
戶已達160萬戶。3Q26特斯拉FSD活躍用戶數已達174萬、年增67%，且北美新車FSD採用率超過75%。
相較傳統車商低於10%的硬體銷售利潤率，軟體毛利率高達50~70%，且訂閱制使車商部分營收轉為持續
性收入，將帶動其估值表現。

美國9月份輕型車年化銷售量約為
1,600萬輛，低於市場預估共識的
1,630萬輛。儘管9月初的勞動節
促銷活動使購車回饋及零售銷售
增加，但同時銷售量年減4%也顯
示市場需求正面臨更大挑戰。
今年上半年儘管面臨高利率、高
車價等負面影響，但高收入族群
支撐使美國車市仍具韌性。然而
隨著利率上升、對消費者可負擔
性的懷疑日益增加，市場目前正
逐步將2026年美國輕型車銷售量
預估由1,610萬輛下修至1,560至
1,600萬輛區間。
聯準會在9月升息一碼，使美國
目標利率區間上升至3.75~4%，
且目前市場普遍預期聯準會在今
年12月及2027年上半年將再各升
息一碼。美國約有75~85%新車
購買使用貸款或融資，利率持續
上升將直接推高車貸利率，進而
增加消費者的購車成本與負債壓
力，且利率上升也會造成核貸門
檻轉趨嚴格，使消費者對新車的
需求下降。
未來若油價、通膨下降，市場重

新步入降息循環後有望使美國車
市需求重新回升。資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/9/24，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/22

福特、通用汽車全尺寸皮卡在改款當年及之後
兩年銷售表現優於同業
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，2026/10/13，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/22
車商將迎來皮卡改款週期
回饋優惠在交易價中佔比美國新車交易價格美國新車平均交易價格( ATP )在
9月超過5萬美元，車價上漲在每

年下半年為常見現象，以因應車
款更新而重新定價。然而，9月
ATP年增率低於1%遠低於典型的
9月增幅2~4%，顯示利率上升後
平價車款銷售相對強勁。
儘管美國新車售價成長幅度趨緩，
但其上升趨勢仍使美國傳統車商
利潤率逐步改善。通用、福特汽
車等傳統車廠近年轉向生產更多
卡車和SUV，因部分SUV和皮卡
的調整後息稅前利潤率超過20%，
使得車廠利潤率能維持一定水準。
(美元)
福特與通用汽車即將在2026年年
底至2027年迎來全尺寸皮卡改款
與 更 新 計 畫 ， 福 特 旗 艦 皮 卡 F-
150將換上全新3.0升V6引擎，通
用汽車則將對GMC Sierra 及
Chevrolet Silverado全尺寸皮卡
進行大改款。
鑑於福特與通用汽車在皮卡銷售
中取得的較高利潤，皮卡市場對
獲利的重要性可想而知。由於原
廠在推出新款或新一代卡車後，
通常會有2~3年較佳的銷售期，
預估福特和通用汽車在2027年及
之後的獲利有機會持續提升。

4
高油價使美國電動車銷售逐步回溫
資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/10/12，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28
需求使油電、電動車並存
康寧儘管政策轉向支持燃油車發展，
但自美伊戰爭後油價居高不下，
使油電混合車3Q26在美國的市佔
率暴衝至16%，包括豐田、現代
等品牌在美國的市占率持續上升。

而儘管美國3Q26電動車銷量較去
年同期下降(3Q25為電動車補助
期限，基期較高)，但仍較2Q26
成長近8%，約佔美國新車總銷量
的6.5%，高於2Q26的5.8%，顯
示電動車在美國市場正重新站穩
腳步。以市場反應來看，美國車
市正從先前單一電動化路線，轉
向燃油、油電、電動車並存局面。
美國國會在2025年9/30 正式取
消購買或租賃新電動車的7,500
美元稅收抵免，造成美國電動車
銷售在4Q25開始大幅下滑。另
外，川普政府在今年9月底正式
宣布放寬美國企業燃油效率標準，
將未來新車的燃油效率年均提升
目標下調，使部分車商開始降低
電動車生產以減少虧損。
傳統車企如福特、通用汽車的電
動車部門EBIT利潤率可能介於-
10~-30%以下，燃油效率標準放
寬使其生產方向轉回燃油車發展，
也有助於改善整體獲利結構。

通用汽車遞延收入上升，顯示未來軟體營收增加
資料來源：公司，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/29
軟體營收增加帶動獲利表現
各車商正積極擴大自駕與軟體訂閱
服務布局，並將其視為未來重要獲
利成長動能。 通 用 汽 車 預 估 其
OnStar和Super Cruise自駕輔助營

收在2026年將突破30億美元，並
在未來維持雙位數成長。福特汽車
的軟體付費訂閱用戶已達160萬戶。
3Q26特斯拉FSD活躍用戶數已達
174 萬 、 年增67% ， 且 北 美 新 車
FSD採用率超過75%。
相較傳統車商低於10%的硬體銷售
利潤率，軟體毛利率高達50~70%，
且訂閱制使車商部分營收轉為持續
性收入，將帶動其估值表現。
美國消費者對自動駕駛的認識與接
受度正持續提升。過去十年來，對
自駕車有較多了解的民眾比例由
40%增至58%，且近半數消費者相
信在有生之年將能看到自駕車普及。
另外，消費者雖然支持車輛自動化
發展，但仍傾向保有人工接管能力，
因此對Level 4 的 興 趣 最 高 ， 對
Level 5的興趣反而下降。
調查也發現，多數曾搭乘Robotaxi
的使用者對自動駕駛的觀感明顯改
善，未來隨著服務覆蓋範圍持續擴
大，Robotaxi有望進一步提升大眾
對自駕技術的信心與接受度。 資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/9/28，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/29

谷歌廣告業務短期逆風壓抑指數

市場投資展望
《通訊服務龍頭短期營運雜音較多》
臉書 FY26Q3 財報營收、獲利皆優於預期。管理層
對 AI 投資變現的論述較上季更具體。AI 對廣告價
量的優化仍提供營收成長上行空間，但 Muse 規模
擴張與變現路徑短期變數仍多。谷歌同季財報反映
谷歌業務雙線推展的營運壓力。谷歌雲年增雖令人
驚豔，但搜尋業務營收出於預期的減速引發對做為
現金牛的搜尋業務競爭力的擔憂。隨著 AI Mode 相
關廣告業務全球推展、TPU 系統銷售爬坡與第三方
算力橋接陸續開始，谷歌短期雜音漸增。本行認為
兩龍頭短期上行動能較有限。但另一方面，本行認

為估值已反映大部分的逆風，與歷史估值區間對比
向下修正空間有限。因此，看法仍維持中立。
• 臉書 FY26Q3 財報優於預期，
Meta Muse 的投資變現較上
季更具體，但仍待相關廣告變
現實例與規模展開。
• 谷歌 FY26Q3 財報反映谷歌搜
尋、雲運算業務雙線推展的壓
力。市場將更加聚焦檢視雙線
的業務成長與獲利能力動態。
• 谷歌在傳統搜尋與 AI Mode /
AI Overview 搜尋廣告之間的
最佳化配置這關鍵議題上，將
迎來較多的不確定性。

展的壓力，同時搜尋廣告正迎來較多的不確定性。維持中立。
